# Carbides

Units: T is in K; $Cp^{\circ}$ and $S^{\circ}$ are in cal/mol·K; $H^{\circ} - H_{298}^{\circ}$ , $\Delta Hf^{\circ}$ and $\Delta Gf^{\circ}$ are in kcal/mol.

Sources of data from general references are listed below, with the reference(s) given immediately after each formula:

$\mathrm{AlC(g)}$ (7), $\mathrm{BC(g)}$ (7), $\mathrm{B_4C(c,l)}$ (30), $\mathrm{BaC_2(c)}$ (1, 26), $\mathrm{Be_2C(c,l)}$ (26, 28), $\mathrm{CaC_2(c)}$ (26), $\mathrm{CeC_2(c)}$ (1), $\mathrm{Ce_2C_3(c)}$ (2), $\mathrm{Li_2C_2(c)}$ (7), $\mathrm{MgC_2(c)}$ (7, 26), $\mathrm{Mg_2C_3(c)}$ (7, 26), $\mathrm{Mn_3C(c)}$ (31), $\mathrm{MoC(c)}$ (2), $\mathrm{NbC_{0.98}(c)}$ (6), $\mathrm{Nb_2C(c)}$ (28, 32), $\mathrm{Ni_3C(c)}$ (1), $\mathrm{SiC(α)}$ (30), $\mathrm{SiC(β)}$ (30), $\mathrm{SiC(g)}$ (7), $\mathrm{SiC_2(g)}$ (7), $\mathrm{Si_2C(g)}$ (7), $\mathrm{SrC_2(c)}$ (1), $\mathrm{TaC(c)}$ (6), $\mathrm{Ta_2C(c)}$ (28, 32), $\mathrm{ThC_{1.94}(c)}$ (33), $\mathrm{TiC(c)}$ (32), $\mathrm{UC_{1.94}(c)}$ (33), $\mathrm{VC_{0.88}(c)}$ (32), $\mathrm{W_2C(c)}$ (28).
